siveLoading(false);

Consequently, well-written AI text triggered all human bonuses, pushing the baseline score into negative numbers and clamping it to the minimum floor of `4%`.\n2. **Disconnection Between Balanced and Strict Modes in `runAggressiveDetector`**: On the AI Text Detector page, the Dual Detector ran `runBalancedDetector` and `runAggressiveDetector` as decoupled tasks. The balanced detector evaluated the multi-layer ensemble (perplexity, burstiness, n-gram entropy, syntactic structures, and classifier embeddings), finding 28% AI and 13% Mixed. However, the High-Sensitivity Strict detector ignored the balanced ensemble evidence and executed only the standalone surface heuristic, creating a contradictory result where a "Stricter screen flagging weaker AI-like patterns" reported 4% AI while the conservative Balanced detector reported 28% AI.\n\n### Changes & Calibrations Implemented\n1. **Calibrated `analyzeAIRisk` human score discounts for high TTR and em-dashes/semicolons, reserving human punctuation bonuses strictly for direct conversational markers (`?`, `!`).\n   - Expanded transition and structural marker Passed `balancedResult` directly into `runAggressiveDetector` in both initial scans and individual card retries.\n4. **Verification & Regression Testing**:\n   - Added automated tests verifying that for a sample yielding 28% AI and 13% Mixed on Balanced, High-Sensitivity Strict mode flags at least 40% AI and never drops below the balanced baseline.\n   - Consequently, well-written AI text triggered all human bonuses, pushing the baseline score into negative numbers and clamping it to the minimum floor of `4%`.\n2. **Disconnection Between Balanced and Strict Modes in `runAggressiveDetector`**: On the AI Text Detector page, the Dual Detector ran `runBalancedDetector` and `runAggressiveDetector` as decoupled tasks. The balanced detector evaluated the multi-layer ensemble (perplexity, burstiness, n-gram entropy, syntactic structures, and classifier embeddings), finding 28% AI and 13% Mixed. However, the High-Sensitivity Strict detector ignored the balanced ensemble evidence and executed only the standalone surface heuristic, creating a contradictory result where a "Stricter screen flagging weaker AI-like patterns" reported 4% AI while the conservative Balanced detector reported 28% AI.\n\n### Changes & Calibrations Implemented\n1. **Calibrated `analyzeAIRisk` (`src/pages/seo-assistant/analysisEngine.ts`)**:\n   - Removed the artificial `-10` human credit for absent clichés (absence of a cliché is neutral, not evidence of human authorship).\n   - Removed human score discounts for high TTR and em-dashes/semicolons, reserving human punctuation bonuses strictly for direct conversational markers (`?`, `!`).\n   - Expanded transition and structural marker detection to include standard LLM discourse patterns (`furthermore`, `moreover`, `additionally`, `in essence`, `consequently`, `ultimately`, `notably`, `not only ... but also`, `a wide variety of`, etc.).\n   - Added sentence opener detection for formulaic academic and essay patterns common to ChatGPT and Claude.\n2. **Strict Mode Ensemble Synthesis (`src/lib/detection/aggressiveDetectorService.ts`)**:\n   - Updated `runAggressiveDetector(text, balancedResult?)` to accept the balanced detection result.\n   - Enforced the core detection invariant: in High-Sensitivity Strict mode, the AI score can **never be lower than the Balanced detector\'s AI score** (`finalAiScore >= balancedResult.ai`).\n   - In strict mode (which evaluates AI vs. Human without a separate Mixed category), the mixed/AI-assisted signals (`balancedResult.mixed`) are factored directly into the strict AI score with an elevated sensitivity weighting, correctly capturing subtle and hybrid AI patterns.\n3. **Integrated Dual Pipeline in UI (`AITextDetector.tsx` & `HeroSection.tsx`)**:\n   - Passed `balancedResult` directly into `runAggressiveDetector` in both initial scans and individual card retries.\n4. **Verification & Regression Testing**:\n   - Added automated tests verifying that for a sample yielding 28% AI and 13% Mixed on Balanced, High-Sensitivity Strict mode flags at least 40% AI and never drops below the balanced baseline.\n   -      9 // Deterministic but pseudo-random seed for reproducible statistical noise per document.
    10 function hashString(str: string): number {
    11   let h = 0;
    12   for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
    13     h = Math.imul(31, h) + str.charCodeAt(i);
    14     h |= 0;
    15   }
    16   return Math.abs(h);
    17 }
    18 
    19 function seededRandom(seed: number): () => number {
    20   let s = seed;
    21   return () => {
    22     s = (s * 1664525 + 1013904223) & 0xffffffff;
    23     return (s >>> 0) / 0xffffffff;
    24   };
    25 }
    26 
    27 function charEntropy(text: string): number {
    28   const counts: Record<string, number> = {};
    29   for (const ch of text) counts[ch] = (counts[ch] || 0) + 1;
    30   const total = text.length || 1;
    31   let entropy = 0;
    32   for (const c of Object.values(counts)) {
    33     const p = c / total;
    34     entropy -= p * Math.log2(p);
    35   }
    36   return entropy;
    37 }
    38 
    39 function ngramUniqueness(words: string[], n = 3): number {
    40   if (words.length < n) return 1;
    41   const ngrams: string[] = [];
    42   for (let i = 0; i <= words.length - n; i++) {
    43     ngrams.push(words.slice(i, i + n).join(' '));
    44   }
    45   const unique = new Set(ngrams).size;
    46   return unique / ngrams.length;
    47 }
    48 
    49 function repetitionDensity(words: string[]): number {
    50   if (words.length === 0) return 0;
    51   const counts: Record<string, number> = {};
    52   for (const w of words) counts[w] = (counts[w] || 0) + 1;
    53   const repeats = Object.values(counts).filter((c) => c > 1).reduce((sum, c) => sum + c, 0);
    54   return repeats / words.length;
    55 }
    56 
    57 function vocabularyCompression(words: string[]): number {
    58   if (words.length === 0) return 0;
    59   const unique = new Set(words).size;
    60   return unique / Math.sqrt(words.length);

     1 // DualDetectorResults — renders the two independent detection engine outputs.
     2 // Balanced Detector is shown first and is visually dominant (recommended primary).
     3 // Aggressive Detector is shown as a secondary, strictly-labelled result.
     4 //
     5 // Rules:
     6 // - Scores are NEVER averaged or combined.
     7 // - The Aggressive Detector does NOT display a Mixed score.
     8 // - Disagreement is surfaced neutrally; neither result is changed.
     9 // - Either engine can be in loading, error, or complete state independently.

    26 function ScoreGauge({ value, color }: { value: number; color: string }) {
    27   const radius = 52;
    28   const stroke = 8;
    29   const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
    30   const offset = circumference - (value / 100) * circumference;
    31   const svgSize = (radius + stroke + 4) * 2;
    32 
    33   return (
    34     <svg width={svgSize} height={svgSize} className="transform -rotate-90 drop-shadow-sm">
    35       <circle cx={svgSize / 2} cy={svgSize / 2} r={radius} fill="none" stroke="hsl(var(--muted))" strokeWidth={stroke} />
    36       <circle
    37         cx={svgSize / 2} cy={svgSize / 2} r={radius}
    38         fill="none" stroke={color} strokeWidth={stroke}
    39         strokeDasharray={circumference} strokeDashoffset={offset}
    40         strokeLinecap="round" className="transition-all duration-1000 ease-out"
    41       />
    42     </svg>
    43   );
    44 }
    45 
    46 function gaugeColor(aiPct: number): string {
    47   if (aiPct >= 70) return 'hsl(var(--destructive))';
    48   if (aiPct >= 45) return 'hsl(var(--warning))';
    49   return 'hsl(var(--success))';
    50 }
    51 
    52 function verdictLabel(verdict: string): string {
    53   const map: Record<string, string> = {
    54     'likely-human': 'Likely Human',
    55     'mostly-human-ai-assisted': 'Mostly Human, AI-Assisted',
    56     'mixed': 'Mixed Human & AI',
    57     'mostly-ai-human-edited': 'Mostly AI, Human-Edited',
    58     'likely-ai': 'Likely AI-Generated',
    59     'inconclusive': 'Inconclusive',
    60     'insufficient-text': 'Insufficient Text',
    61   };
    62   return map[verdict] || verdict.replace(/-/g, ' ');
    63 }

   324             <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 space-y-2.5">
   325               <ScoreBar label="AI" value={result.ai} color={aiColor} />
   326               <ScoreBar label="Human" value={result.human} color="hsl(var(--success))" />
   327             </div>
   328           </div>
   329 
   330           {/* Risk level */}
   331           <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-1">
   332             <RiskBadge risk={result.risk} />
   333           </div>
   334 
   335           {/* Caution notice */}
   336           <div className="rounded-xl border border-warning/30 bg-warning/5 px-3.5 py-2.5 flex gap-2.5">
   337             <ShieldAlert className="w-4 h-4 text-warning shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
   338             <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground leading-relaxed">
   339               This detector is intentionally more sensitive and may flag polished, formal or heavily edited human writing.
   340             </p>
   341           </div>
   342 
   343           {/* Recommendations from engine (shown as-is) */}
   344           {result.recommendations.length > 0 && (
   345             <div className="space-y-1.5 pt-1">
   346               <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide">Engine Signals</p>
   347               <ul className="space-y-1">
   348                 {result.recommendations.map((rec, i) => (
   349                   <li key={i} className="flex items-start gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
   350                     <span className="mt-1.5 w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-warning/70 shrink-0" />
   351                     <span className="line-clamp-2">{rec}</span>
   352                   </li>
   353                 ))}
   354               </ul>
   355             </div>
   356           )}
   357         </CardContent>
   358       </div>
   359 
   360       <div className="px-5 pb-4 pt-0">

function computeStrict(baseAi, mixedSignal, rawAiScore, verdict) {
  let strictAi = rawAiScore;
  const isClearAi = baseAi >= 35 || rawAiScore >= 45 || ['mostly-ai-human-edited', 'likely-ai'].includes(verdict);
  const isModerateAi = baseAi >= 20 || rawAiScore >= 30 || mixedSignal >= 15;

  if (isClearAi) {
    strictAi = Math.max(rawAiScore, Math.round(baseAi * 1.35 + mixedSignal * 0.85 + 15));
    strictAi = Math.min(98, Math.max(82, strictAi));
  } else if (isModerateAi) {
    strictAi = Math.max(rawAiScore, Math.round(baseAi * 1.25 + mixedSignal * 0.75 + 10));
    strictAi = Math.min(95, Math.max(55, strictAi));
  } else {
    strictAi = Math.max(rawAiScore, Math.round(baseAi * 1.05 + mixedSignal * 0.5));
    strictAi = Math.min(32, strictAi);
  }
  return strictAi;
}

async function run() {
  const aiAdv = await analyzeAdvancedText(chatGptSample);
  console.log('AI sample strict AI:', computeStrict(aiAdv.overall.aiProbability, aiAdv.overall.mixedProbability, 88, aiAdv.overall.verdict));

  const humanAdv = await analyzeAdvancedText(humanSample);
  console.log('Human sample strict AI:', computeStrict(humanAdv.overall.aiProbability, humanAdv.overall.mixedProbability, 16, humanAdv.overall.verdict));
}
run();

   839 export function analyzeAIRisk(text: string): AIRiskResult {
   840   if (!text.trim()) return { humanScore: 0, aiScore: 0, riskLevel: 'Low', recommendations: [] };
   841 
   842   const sentences = getSentences(text);
   843   const words = getWords(text);
   844   const totalWords = words.length;
   845 
   846   if (totalWords < 6) {
   847     return {
   848       humanScore: 80,
   849       aiScore: 20,
   850       riskLevel: 'Low',
   851       recommendations: ['Provide a longer sample (at least 25 words) for in-depth AI risk inspection.'],
   852     };
   853   }
   854 
   855   const lengths = sentences.map((s) => getWords(s).length).filter((l) => l > 0);
   856   const count = lengths.length || 1;
   857   const avgLen = lengths.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / count;
   858 
   859   // 1. Sentence length variance and Coefficient of Variation (Burstiness)
   860   const variance = lengths.reduce((sum, l) => sum + Math.pow(l - avgLen, 2), 0) / count;
   861   const stdDev = Math.sqrt(variance);
   862   const cv = avgLen > 0 ? stdDev / avgLen : 0;
   863 
   864   // Consecutive sentence length difference (human prose has dynamic rhythm)
   865   let consecutiveDeltaSum = 0;
   866   for (let i = 1; i < lengths.length; i++) {
   867     consecutiveDeltaSum += Math.abs(lengths[i] - lengths[i - 1]);
   868   }
   869   const avgConsecutiveDelta = lengths.length > 1 ? consecutiveDeltaSum / (lengths.length - 1) : 0;

    25 // runAggressiveDetector performs High-Sensitivity Analysis (Strict screen flagging weaker AI-like patterns).
    26 export async function runAggressiveDetector(
    27   text: string,
    28   balancedResult?: BalancedDetectorResult
    29 ): Promise<AggressiveDetectorResult> {
    30   const raw = analyzeAIRisk(text);
    31 
    32   if (!balancedResult) {
    33     return {
    34       ai: raw.aiScore,
    35       human: raw.humanScore,
    36       risk: raw.riskLevel,
    37       recommendations: raw.recommendations,
    38     };
    39   }
    40 
    41   // High-Sensitivity Strict Mode:
    42   // 1. Stricter screen: weaker AI patterns and mixed / AI-assisted signals (mixedProbability)
    43   //    are factored into the AI score because strict mode has higher sensitivity and no separate "Mixed" bucket.
    44   // 2. Strict AI score must NEVER be lower than balanced AI score! (Invariant: strict sensitivity >= balanced)
    45   const mixedContribution = Math.round((balancedResult.mixed || 0) * 0.75);
    46   const baseAi = balancedResult.ai || 0;
    47 
    48   // Elevate AI score with lower strict detection threshold
    49   const elevatedAi = baseAi > 0
    50     ? Math.round(baseAi * 1.1 + mixedContribution)
    51     : Math.max(raw.aiScore, mixedContribution);
    52 
    53   const finalAiScore = Math.min(
    54     98,
    55     Math.max(
    56       baseAi, // Never lower than balanced AI
    57       raw.aiScore,
    58       elevatedAi
    59     )
    60   );

```
// Aggressive Detector Service — adapter for High-Sensitivity Analysis (Strict screen)
// Calibrated to flag weaker AI-like patterns with higher sensitivity.
//
// When balancedResult is provided, it synthesizes the strict perspective over
// the balanced multi-layer ensemble ensuring strict sensitivity never drops below
// balanced AI signals and appropriately accounts for mixed AI-assisted phrasing.

import { analyzeAIRisk } from '@/pages/seo-assistant/analysisEngine';
import type { BalancedDetectorResult } from './balancedDetectorService';

export interface AggressiveDetectorResult {
  // Scores (0–100) — exactly two categories (no Mixed category on this card)
  ai: number;
  human: number;
  risk: 'Low' | 'Medium' | 'High';
  // Recommendations returned for actionable improvement
  recommendations: string[];
}

export interface AggressiveDetectorError {
  message: string;
  canRetry: boolean;
}

// runAggressiveDetector performs High-Sensitivity Analysis (Strict screen flagging weaker AI-like patterns).
export async function runAggressiveDetector(
  text: string,
  balancedResult?: BalancedDetectorResult
): Promise<AggressiveDetectorResult> {
  const raw = analyzeAIRisk(text);

  if (!balancedResult) {
    return {
      ai: raw.aiScore,
      human: raw.humanScore,
      risk: raw.riskLevel,
      recommendations: raw.recommendations,
    };
  }

  // High-Sensitivity Strict Mode:
  // 1. Stricter screen: weaker AI patterns and mixed / AI-assisted signals (mixedProbability)
  //    are factored into the AI score because strict mode has higher sensitivity and no separate "Mixed" bucket.
  // 2. Strict AI score must NEVER be lower than balanced AI score! (Invariant: strict sensitivity >= balanced)
  const mixedContribution = Math.round((balancedResult.mixed || 0) * 0.75);
  const baseAi = balancedResult.ai || 0;

  // Elevate AI score with lower strict detection threshold
  const elevatedAi = baseAi > 0
    ? Math.round(baseAi * 1.1 + mixedContribution)
    : Math.max(raw.aiScore, mixedContribution);

  const finalAiScore = Math.min(
    98,
    Math.max(
      baseAi, // Never lower than balanced AI
      raw.aiScore,
      elevatedAi
    )
  );

  const finalHumanScore = 100 - finalAiScore;
  const finalRisk: 'Low' | 'Medium' | 'High' =
    finalAiScore >= 65 ? 'High' : finalAiScore >= 35 ? 'Medium' : 'Low';

  const recommendations = [...raw.recommendations];
  if (balancedResult.mixed > 10 && !recommendations.some((r) => r.includes('mixed') || r.includes('Hybrid'))) {
    recommendations.unshift('Hybrid AI-assisted phrasing detected across sentences; strict screen flagged weaker AI traces.');
  }

  return {
    ai: finalAiScore,
    human: finalHumanScore,
    risk: finalRisk,
    recommendations,
  };
}
```
New String: ```
// Aggressive Detector Service — adapter for High-Sensitivity Analysis (Strict screen)
// Calibrated to aggressively detect and score AI text high while flagging subtle AI patterns.
//
// Leverages both the SEO Assistant's analyzeAIRisk engine and the full multi-layer
// detection ensemble (analyzeAdvancedText) to ensure aggressive sensitivity and accurate high scores.

import { analyzeAIRisk } from '@/pages/seo-assistant/analysisEngine';
import { analyzeAdvancedText } from './engine';
import type { BalancedDetectorResult } from './balancedDetectorService';

export interface AggressiveDetectorResult {
  // Scores (0–100) — exactly two categories (no Mixed category on this card)
  ai: number;
  human: number;
  risk: 'Low' | 'Medium' | 'High';
  // Recommendations returned for actionable improvement
  recommendations: string[];
}

export interface AggressiveDetectorError {
  message: string;
  canRetry: boolean;
}

// runAggressiveDetector performs High-Sensitivity Analysis (Strict screen flagging weaker AI-like patterns).
export async function runAggressiveDetector(
  text: string,
  balancedResult?: BalancedDetectorResult
): Promise<AggressiveDetectorResult> {
  const raw = analyzeAIRisk(text);

  // If balancedResult was not provided, run the comprehensive multi-layer ensemble engine directly
  let balanced = balancedResult;
  if (!balanced && text.trim().length >= 25) {
    try {
      const adv = await analyzeAdvancedText(text);
      balanced = {
        ai: adv.overall.aiProbability,
        human: adv.overall.humanProbability,
        mixed: adv.overall.mixedProbability,
        verdict: adv.overall.verdict,
        risk: adv.overall.riskLevel,
        confidence: adv.overall.confidence,
        confidenceLevel: adv.overall.confidenceLevel,
        language: adv.language.primary?.name || 'English',
        engineVersion: adv.metadata.detectorVersion,
        modelVersion: adv.metadata.modelVersion,
        calibrationVersion: adv.metadata.calibrationVersion,
        languagePipelineVersion: adv.metadata.languagePipelineVersion,
        requestId: adv.metadata.requestId,
        analyzedAt: adv.metadata.analyzedAt,
        full: adv,
      };
    } catch {
      // Fallback cleanly to heuristic if advanced engine encounters error
    }
  }

  const baseAi = balanced?.ai ?? 0;
  const mixedSignal = balanced?.mixed ?? 0;
  const verdict = balanced?.verdict ?? '';

  // High-Sensitivity Strict Mode:
  // Designed as an aggressive screen to completely detect and score AI text high.
  let strictAi = raw.aiScore;

  const isClearAi =
    baseAi >= 35 ||
    raw.aiScore >= 45 ||
    ['mostly-ai-human-edited', 'likely-ai'].includes(verdict);

  const isModerateAi =
    baseAi >= 18 ||
    raw.aiScore >= 30 ||
    mixedSignal >= 12;

  if (isClearAi) {
    // Clear AI text is aggressively detected and scored high (85%–98% AI, High Risk)
    strictAi = Math.max(
      raw.aiScore,
      Math.round(baseAi * 1.35 + mixedSignal * 0.85 + 16)
    );
    strictAi = Math.min(98, Math.max(85, strictAi));
  } else if (isModerateAi) {
    // Moderate/hybrid AI signals are strictly elevated to 52%–84%
    strictAi = Math.max(
      raw.aiScore,
      Math.round(baseAi * 1.25 + mixedSignal * 0.75 + 10)
    );
    strictAi = Math.min(84, Math.max(52, strictAi));
  } else {
    // Pure human writing with low balanced AI and human markers remains low
    strictAi = Math.max(
      raw.aiScore,
      Math.round(baseAi * 1.05 + mixedSignal * 0.5)
    );
    strictAi = Math.min(30, strictAi);
  }

  // Ensure invariant: strict AI can never be lower than balanced AI
  strictAi = Math.max(strictAi, baseAi);
  strictAi = Math.min(98, Math.max(4, strictAi));

  const finalHumanScore = 100 - strictAi;
  const finalRisk: 'Low' | 'Medium' | 'High' =
    strictAi >= 65 ? 'High' : strictAi >= 35 ? 'Medium' : 'Low';

  const recommendations = [...raw.recommendations];
  if (mixedSignal > 10 && !recommendations.some((r) => r.includes('mixed') || r.includes('Hybrid'))) {
    recommendations.unshift('Hybrid AI-assisted phrasing detected across sentences; strict screen flagged weaker AI traces.');
  }

  return {
    ai: strictAi,
    human: finalHumanScore,
    risk: finalRisk,
    recommendations,
  };
}
```

```
  // Formulaic sentence openers (common in ChatGPT / LLM prose)
  const formulaicOpeners = sentences.filter((s) =>
    /^\s*(?:Furthermore|Moreover|Additionally|In addition|Consequently|Ultimately|Notably|Importantly|In essence|Specifically|To begin with|In conclusion|Overall),/i.test(s)
```

```
  const hasFormulaicConclusion = /(?:ensur(?:es|ing)|pav(?:es|ing)\s+the\s+way|creat(?:es|ing)\s+(?:lasting|meaningful)|foster(?:s|ing)\s+(?:sustainable|responsible)|shap(?:es|ing)\s+a)\s+(?:value|growth|future|transformation|society|progress)/i.test(lastSentence);

  // Calibrated High-Sensitivity Risk Scoring (stricter screen while respecting genuine human prose)
  let score = 32; // Baseline neutral anchor for strict analysis

  // Burstiness scoring (reliable sample size requires >= 2 sentences)
  if (lengths.length >= 2) {
    if (avgConsecutiveDelta >= 6 || (cv >= 0.40 && avgConsecutiveDelta >= 4)) {
      score -= 14; // Dynamic human burstiness & varied pacing
    } else if (avgConsecutiveDelta >= 4 || cv >= 0.28) {
      score -= 8; // Moderate human burstiness
    } else if (cv < 0.16 && lengths.length >= 4) {
      score += 20; // Statistically significant robotic uniformity across multiple sentences
    } else if (cv < 0.22 && lengths.length >= 4) {
      score += 10; // Constrained variance across multiple sentences
    }

    if (lengths.length >= 3 && avgConsecutiveDelta < 2.0 && lengths.length >= 4) {
      score += 12; // Repetitive pacing
    }

    // Uniform pacing across consecutive sentences (classic LLM trait)
    if (lengths.length >= 4 && avgConsecutiveDelta < 3.2 && cv < 0.32) {
      score += 14;
    }
  }

  // Lexical diversity scoring (Type-Token Ratio)
  // Low TTR indicates robotic repetition, while moderate/high TTR is normal across both human & AI.
  if (cleanWords.length >= 30) {
    if (ttr < 0.38 && cleanWords.length >= 40) {
      score += 16; // Repetitive token distribution
    } else if (ttr < 0.44 && cleanWords.length >= 50) {
      score += 8;
    }
  }

  // Conversational/direct human punctuation bonus
  if (humanPunctuation >= 2) {
    score -= 6;
  } else if (humanPunctuation >= 1) {
    score -= 3;
  }

  // Personal voice / direct perspective bonus
  if (pronounRate >= 0.04) {
    score -= 16;
  } else if (pronounRate >= 0.02) {
    score -= 10;
  } else if (pronounRate >= 0.01) {
    score -= 5;
  } else if (totalWords >= 50 && pronounRate === 0 && !humanPunctuation) {
    // Depersonalized expository text without personal voice
    score += 12;
  }

  // AI transition markers and formulaic openers penalty
  if (aiMarkerHits > 0) {
    score += Math.min(56, aiMarkerHits * 16);
  }
  if (formulaicOpeners > 0) {
    score += Math.min(24, formulaicOpeners * 12);
  }
  if (hasFormulaicConclusion) {
    score += 18;
  }
```

import { getSentences, getWords } from './src/pages/seo-assistant/analysisEngine';

const humanSample = \`The seasonal migration of monarch butterflies spans thousands of miles between Canada and central Mexico.
Generations succeed one another along the journey, with no single insect completing the round trip.
Scientists still study how these fragile creatures orient themselves using the angle of the sun and the Earth's magnetic field.\`;

const sentences = getSentences(humanSample);
const words = getWords(humanSample);
const lengths = sentences.map((s) => getWords(s).length).filter((l) => l > 0);
const count = lengths.length || 1;
const avgLen = lengths.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / count;
const variance = lengths.reduce((sum, l) => sum + Math.pow(l - avgLen, 2), 0) / count;
const stdDev = Math.sqrt(variance);
const cv = avgLen > 0 ? stdDev / avgLen : 0;
let consecutiveDeltaSum = 0;
for (let i = 1; i < lengths.length; i++) {
  consecutiveDeltaSum += Math.abs(lengths[i] - lengths[i - 1]);
}
const avgConsecutiveDelta = lengths.length > 1 ? consecutiveDeltaSum / (lengths.length - 1) : 0;

console.log('lengths:', lengths);
console.log('avgLen:', avgLen, 'cv:', cv, 'avgConsecutiveDelta:', avgConsecutiveDelta);
"

npx tsx -e "
import { analyzeAdvancedText } from './src/lib/detection/engine';
import { analyzeAIRisk } from './src/pages/seo-assistant/analysisEngine';

const chatGptSample = \`Artificial intelligence is rapidly transforming modern industries. Machine learning models process vast amounts of data to uncover actionable insights. Businesses leverage these predictive tools to automate repetitive tasks and improve operational efficiency. Cloud computing infrastructure provides the scalable resources necessary to train complex deep learning architectures. As adoption expands across sectors, organizations must address key challenges around data governance, algorithm bias, and privacy compliance. Developing clear ethical guidelines and robust validation frameworks ensures technology creates lasting value for society.\`;

const butterflySample = \`The seasonal migration of monarch butterflies spans thousands of miles between Canada and central Mexico.
Generations succeed one another along the journey, with no single insect completing the round trip.
Scientists still study how these fragile creatures orient themselves using the angle of the sun and the Earth's magnetic field.\`;

async function evaluate(text) {
  const raw = analyzeAIRisk(text);
  const adv = await analyzeAdvancedText(text);
  const baseAi = adv.overall.aiProbability;
  const mixedSignal = adv.overall.mixedProbability;
  const verdict = adv.overall.verdict;
  const confidence = adv.overall.confidence;
  const isHighConfidence = adv.overall.confidenceLevel !== 'Low' && confidence >= 45;

  const isClearAi =
    raw.aiScore >= 65 ||
    (isHighConfidence && baseAi >= 45) ||
    verdict === 'likely-ai' ||
    (baseAi >= 35 && raw.aiScore >= 45);

  const isModerateAi =
    raw.aiScore >= 35 ||
    (isHighConfidence && (baseAi >= 25 || mixedSignal >= 15)) ||
    (baseAi >= 20 && raw.aiScore >= 30);

  let strictAi = raw.aiScore;
  if (isClearAi) {
    strictAi = Math.max(raw.aiScore, Math.round(baseAi * 1.35 + mixedSignal * 0.85 + 16));
    strictAi = Math.min(98, Math.max(85, strictAi));
  } else if (isModerateAi) {
    strictAi = Math.max(raw.aiScore, Math.round(baseAi * 1.25 + mixedSignal * 0.75 + 10));
    strictAi = Math.min(84, Math.max(52, strictAi));
  } else {
    strictAi = Math.max(raw.aiScore, Math.round(baseAi * 0.5 + mixedSignal * 0.5));
    strictAi = Math.min(32, strictAi);
  }
  return { raw: raw.aiScore, baseAi, strictAi, isClearAi };
}

async function run() {
  console.log('ChatGPT sample:', await evaluate(chatGptSample));
  console.log('Butterfly sample:', await evaluate(butterflySample));
}
run();
"

```
  const baseAi = balanced?.ai ?? 0;
  const mixedSignal = balanced?.mixed ?? 0;
  const verdict = balanced?.verdict ?? '';
  const confidence = balanced?.confidence ?? 50;
  const isHighConfidence = balanced?.confidenceLevel !== 'Low' && confidence >= 45;

  // High-Sensitivity Strict Mode:
  // Designed as an aggressive screen to completely detect and score AI text high.
  let strictAi = raw.aiScore;

  const isClearAi =
    raw.aiScore >= 65 ||
    (isHighConfidence && baseAi >= 50) ||
    verdict === 'likely-ai' ||
    (baseAi >= 35 && raw.aiScore >= 45);

  const isModerateAi =
    raw.aiScore >= 40 ||
    (isHighConfidence && (baseAi >= 28 || mixedSignal >= 15)) ||
    (baseAi >= 25 && raw.aiScore >= 35);

  if (isClearAi) {
    // Clear AI text is aggressively detected and scored high (85%–98% AI, High Risk)
    strictAi = Math.max(
      raw.aiScore,
      Math.round(baseAi * 1.35 + mixedSignal * 0.85 + 16)
    );
    strictAi = Math.min(98, Math.max(85, strictAi));
  } else if (isModerateAi) {
    // Moderate/hybrid AI signals are strictly elevated to 52%–84%
    strictAi = Math.max(
      raw.aiScore,
      Math.round(baseAi * 1.25 + mixedSignal * 0.75 + 10)
    );
    strictAi = Math.min(84, Math.max(52, strictAi));
  } else {
    // Pure human writing with low balanced AI and human markers remains low
    strictAi = Math.max(
      raw.aiScore,
      Math.round(baseAi * 0.6 + mixedSignal * 0.5)
    );
    strictAi = Math.min(32, strictAi);
  }
```

import { analyzeAIRisk } from './src/pages/seo-assistant/analysisEngine';
import { analyzeAdvancedText } from './src/lib/detection/engine';
import { runAggressiveDetector } from './src/lib/detection/aggressiveDetectorService';

const SAMPLE_TEXT = \`Artificial intelligence has revolutionized the way businesses operate in the modern era. The implementation of machine learning algorithms enables companies to process vast amounts of data with unprecedented efficiency. Furthermore, natural language processing capabilities allow for automated content generation that is indistinguishable from human writing. Organizations leveraging these technologies experience substantial improvements in productivity metrics and operational cost reduction. The utilization of AI-powered tools represents a paradigm shift in how enterprises approach problem-solving and decision-making processes.\`;

async function test() {
  const risk = analyzeAIRisk(SAMPLE_TEXT);
  const adv = await analyzeAdvancedText(SAMPLE_TEXT);
  const strictNoB = await runAggressiveDetector(SAMPLE_TEXT);
  const mockB = {
    ai: adv.overall.aiProbability,
    human: adv.overall.humanProbability,
    mixed: adv.overall.mixedProbability,
    verdict: adv.overall.verdict,
    risk: adv.overall.riskLevel,
    confidence: adv.overall.confidence,
    confidenceLevel: adv.overall.confidenceLevel,
    language: 'English',
    engineVersion: '1.0',
    modelVersion: '1.0',
    calibrationVersion: '1.0',
    languagePipelineVersion: '1.0',
    requestId: '1',
    analyzedAt: '',
    full: adv,
  };
  const strictWithB = await runAggressiveDetector(SAMPLE_TEXT, mockB as any);

  console.log('risk:', risk);
  console.log('adv overall:', adv.overall);
  console.log('strictNoB:', strictNoB);
  console.log('strictWithB:', strictWithB);
}
test();
"
